**本章主线：** 生态背景 → 光合作用与遗传 → 萌发开花结果 → 栽培收获 → 保护与结果评价

**最后尝试回答：** 从一粒种子开始，解释它怎样生长、被栽培，并受到环境影响。

### 词群 02.01｜认识植物与生态系统 {#group-02-01}

**串联方式：** 先辨植物、草本和多年生植物，再从植物学进入生态学、园艺和生态友好实践。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-004">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-004" class="vocabulary-word-title">vegetation<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-004" aria-label="定位 vegetation">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-004" data-mark-label="vegetation" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="vegetation：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="vegetation：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vegetation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 vegetation" title="在有道查询 vegetation">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-004</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 植物，草木</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This perennial herb is part of the island's native <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vegetation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vegetation</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种多年生草本是岛上本土植被的一部分。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Restoring native <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vegetation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vegetation</strong></a> helped this perennial herb return to the hillside.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">恢复本土植被帮助这种多年生草本重回山坡。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The survey found a rare perennial herb beneath the dense <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vegetation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vegetation</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查在茂密植被下发现了一种罕见的多年生草本。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">native vegetation</span> · 本土植被</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=annual&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">annual</a></span> · 一年生的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ground%20cover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ground cover</a></span> · 地被植物</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students use <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=botany&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>botany</strong></a> to identify species and ecology to understand their roles in the ecosystem.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生用植物学识别物种，用生态学理解它们在生态系统中的作用。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-010">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-010" class="vocabulary-word-title">eco-friendly<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-010" aria-label="定位 eco-friendly">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-010" data-mark-label="eco-friendly" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="eco-friendly：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="eco-friendly：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eco-friendly&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 eco-friendly" title="在有道查询 eco-friendly">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-010</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 对生态环境友好的，不妨害生态环境的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eco-friendly&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eco-friendly</strong></a> approach to horticulture considers each organism living in the soil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">环保的园艺方法会考虑土壤中的每一种生物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students of horticulture tested whether an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eco-friendly&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>eco-friendly</strong></a> pesticide harmed a beneficial organism.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">园艺学生测试一种环保农药是否会伤害某种有益生物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=eco-friendly&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Eco-friendly</strong></a> horticulture protects soil health instead of treating every unfamiliar organism as a pest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">环保园艺保护土壤健康，而非将每种陌生生物都视为害虫。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">eco-friendly packaging</span> · 环保包装</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sustainable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sustainable</a></span> · 可持续的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cultivation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cultivation</a></span> · 栽培</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-011">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-011" class="vocabulary-word-title">horticulture<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-011" aria-label="定位 horticulture">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-011" data-mark-label="horticulture" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="horticulture：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="horticulture：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=horticulture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 horticulture" title="在有道查询 horticulture">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-011</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 园艺学；园艺</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An eco-friendly approach to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=horticulture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>horticulture</strong></a> considers each organism living in the soil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">环保的园艺方法会考虑土壤中的每一种生物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=horticulture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>horticulture</strong></a> tested whether an eco-friendly pesticide harmed a beneficial organism.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">园艺学生测试一种环保农药是否会伤害某种有益生物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Eco-friendly <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=horticulture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>horticulture</strong></a> protects soil health instead of treating every unfamiliar organism as a pest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">环保园艺保护土壤健康，而非将每种陌生生物都视为害虫。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">urban horticulture</span> · 城市园艺</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sustainable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sustainable</a></span> · 可持续的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cultivation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">cultivation</a></span> · 栽培</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 有机体，生物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An eco-friendly approach to horticulture considers each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=organism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>organism</strong></a> living in the soil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">环保的园艺方法会考虑土壤中的每一种生物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students of horticulture tested whether an eco-friendly pesticide harmed a beneficial <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=organism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>organism</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">园艺学生测试一种环保农药是否会伤害某种有益生物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Eco-friendly horticulture protects soil health instead of treating every unfamiliar <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=organism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>organism</strong></a> as a pest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">环保园艺保护土壤健康，而非将每种陌生生物都视为害虫。</p></li>

### 词群 02.02｜植物如何获取能量 {#group-02-02}

**串联方式：** 从阳光条件进入光合作用和呼吸，再用吸收、释放、酶、养分与循环解释机制。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">photosynthesis<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-001" aria-label="定位 photosynthesis">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-001" data-mark-label="photosynthesis" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="photosynthesis：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="photosynthesis：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=photosynthesis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 photosynthesis" title="在有道查询 photosynthesis">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 光合作用</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Plants use sunlight and carbon dioxide for <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=photosynthesis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>photosynthesis</strong></a>, but they also respire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">植物利用阳光和二氧化碳进行光合作用，但它们也会呼吸。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Although sunlight drives <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=photosynthesis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>photosynthesis</strong></a>, plants respire continuously and release carbon dioxide.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">虽然阳光驱动光合作用，植物仍持续呼吸并释放二氧化碳。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When sunlight fades, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=photosynthesis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>photosynthesis</strong></a> stops, yet the leaves continue to respire and produce carbon dioxide.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">阳光消失时，光合作用停止，但叶片继续呼吸并产生二氧化碳。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">leaf photosynthesis</span> · 叶片光合作用</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chlorophyll&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">chlorophyll</a></span> · 叶绿素</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oxygen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oxygen</a></span> · 氧气</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-002">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-002" class="vocabulary-word-title">respire<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-002" aria-label="定位 respire">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-002" data-mark-label="respire" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="respire：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="respire：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 respire" title="在有道查询 respire">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-002</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 呼吸</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Plants use sunlight and carbon dioxide for photosynthesis, but they also <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>respire</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">植物利用阳光和二氧化碳进行光合作用，但它们也会呼吸。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Although sunlight drives photosynthesis, plants <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>respire</strong></a> continuously and release carbon dioxide.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">虽然阳光驱动光合作用，植物仍持续呼吸并释放二氧化碳。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When sunlight fades, photosynthesis stops, yet the leaves continue to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>respire</strong></a> and produce carbon dioxide.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">阳光消失时，光合作用停止，但叶片继续呼吸并产生二氧化碳。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">respire at night</span> · 夜间呼吸</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chlorophyll&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">chlorophyll</a></span> · 叶绿素</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oxygen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oxygen</a></span> · 氧气</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-003">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-003" class="vocabulary-word-title">dioxide<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-003" aria-label="定位 dioxide">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-003" data-mark-label="dioxide" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dioxide：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dioxide：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dioxide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dioxide" title="在有道查询 dioxide">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-003</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 二氧化物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Plants use sunlight and carbon <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dioxide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dioxide</strong></a> for photosynthesis, but they also respire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">植物利用阳光和二氧化碳进行光合作用，但它们也会呼吸。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Although sunlight drives photosynthesis, plants respire continuously and release carbon <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dioxide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dioxide</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">虽然阳光驱动光合作用，植物仍持续呼吸并释放二氧化碳。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When sunlight fades, photosynthesis stops, yet the leaves continue to respire and produce carbon <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dioxide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dioxide</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">阳光消失时，光合作用停止，但叶片继续呼吸并产生二氧化碳。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">carbon dioxide</span> · 二氧化碳</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chlorophyll&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">chlorophyll</a></span> · 叶绿素</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oxygen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oxygen</a></span> · 氧气</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-039">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-039" class="vocabulary-word-title">sunlight<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-039" aria-label="定位 sunlight">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-039" data-mark-label="sunlight" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sunlight：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sunlight：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sunlight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sunlight" title="在有道查询 sunlight">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-039</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 阳光</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Plants use <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sunlight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sunlight</strong></a> and carbon dioxide for photosynthesis, but they also respire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">植物利用阳光和二氧化碳进行光合作用，但它们也会呼吸。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Although <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sunlight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sunlight</strong></a> drives photosynthesis, plants respire continuously and release carbon dioxide.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">虽然阳光驱动光合作用，植物仍持续呼吸并释放二氧化碳。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sunlight&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sunlight</strong></a> fades, photosynthesis stops, yet the leaves continue to respire and produce carbon dioxide.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">阳光消失时，光合作用停止，但叶片继续呼吸并产生二氧化碳。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">direct sunlight</span> · 直射阳光</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chlorophyll&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">chlorophyll</a></span> · 叶绿素</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oxygen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">oxygen</a></span> · 氧气</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-040">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-040" class="vocabulary-word-title">short-day<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-040" aria-label="定位 short-day">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-040" data-mark-label="short-day" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="short-day：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="short-day：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=short-day&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 short-day" title="在有道查询 short-day">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-040</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 短日照的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=short-day&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>short-day</strong></a> plant is not necessarily shade-tolerant, because day length differs from light intensity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">短日照植物不一定耐阴，因为日照时长不同于光照强度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The greenhouse separates <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=short-day&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>short-day</strong></a> plants from shade-tolerant species requiring different light conditions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">温室将短日照植物与需要不同光照条件的耐阴物种分开。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared flowering in a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=short-day&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>short-day</strong></a> crop with growth in a shade-tolerant herb.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较了一种短日照作物的开花与一种耐阴草本的生长。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">short-day plants</span> · 短日照植物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=photoperiod&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">photoperiod</a></span> · 光周期</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=canopy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">canopy</a></span> · 树冠层</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-041">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-041" class="vocabulary-word-title">shade-tolerant<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-041" aria-label="定位 shade-tolerant">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-041" data-mark-label="shade-tolerant" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="shade-tolerant：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="shade-tolerant：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shade-tolerant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 shade-tolerant" title="在有道查询 shade-tolerant">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-041</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 耐阴的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A short-day plant is not necessarily <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shade-tolerant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shade-tolerant</strong></a>, because day length differs from light intensity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">短日照植物不一定耐阴，因为日照时长不同于光照强度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The greenhouse separates short-day plants from <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shade-tolerant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shade-tolerant</strong></a> species requiring different light conditions.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">温室将短日照植物与需要不同光照条件的耐阴物种分开。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared flowering in a short-day crop with growth in a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shade-tolerant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shade-tolerant</strong></a> herb.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较了一种短日照作物的开花与一种耐阴草本的生长。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">shade-tolerant species</span> · 耐阴物种</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=photoperiod&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">photoperiod</a></span> · 光周期</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=canopy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">canopy</a></span> · 树冠层</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-091">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-091" class="vocabulary-word-title">enzyme<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-091" aria-label="定位 enzyme">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-091" data-mark-label="enzyme" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="enzyme：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="enzyme：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enzyme&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 enzyme" title="在有道查询 enzyme">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-091</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 酶</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enzyme&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enzyme</strong></a> acts as a catalyst in a reaction that can release carbon dioxide, contributing to emission from soil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">酶在可释放二氧化碳的反应中充当催化剂，促成土壤的排放。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The study linked soil carbon emission to an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enzyme&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enzyme</strong></a> serving as a catalyst in processes that release energy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究将土壤碳排放与在释放能量过程中充当催化剂的一种酶联系起来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">By controlling this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enzyme&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enzyme</strong></a>, a natural catalyst, scientists changed the rate of gas release and measured the resulting emission.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家通过控制这种作为天然催化剂的酶改变气体释放速率，并测量由此产生的排放。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an active enzyme</span> · 活性酶</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reaction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reaction</a></span> · 反应</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catalysis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">catalysis</a></span> · 催化作用</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-092">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-092" class="vocabulary-word-title">catalyst<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-092" aria-label="定位 catalyst">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-092" data-mark-label="catalyst" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="catalyst：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="catalyst：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catalyst&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 catalyst" title="在有道查询 catalyst">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-092</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 催化剂；促进因素</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An enzyme acts as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catalyst&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>catalyst</strong></a> in a reaction that can release carbon dioxide, contributing to emission from soil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">酶在可释放二氧化碳的反应中充当催化剂，促成土壤的排放。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The study linked soil carbon emission to an enzyme serving as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catalyst&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>catalyst</strong></a> in processes that release energy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究将土壤碳排放与在释放能量过程中充当催化剂的一种酶联系起来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">By controlling this enzyme, a natural <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catalyst&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>catalyst</strong></a>, scientists changed the rate of gas release and measured the resulting emission.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家通过控制这种作为天然催化剂的酶改变气体释放速率，并测量由此产生的排放。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a biological catalyst</span> · 生物催化剂</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reaction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reaction</a></span> · 反应</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catalysis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">catalysis</a></span> · 催化作用</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.释放；发布；n. 释放；发布</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An enzyme acts as a catalyst in a reaction that can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=release&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>release</strong></a> carbon dioxide, contributing to emission from soil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">酶在可释放二氧化碳的反应中充当催化剂，促成土壤的排放。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The study linked soil carbon emission to an enzyme serving as a catalyst in processes that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=release&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>release</strong></a> energy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究将土壤碳排放与在释放能量过程中充当催化剂的一种酶联系起来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">By controlling this enzyme, a natural catalyst, scientists changed the rate of gas <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=release&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>release</strong></a> and measured the resulting emission.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家通过控制这种作为天然催化剂的酶改变气体释放速率，并测量由此产生的排放。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">release energy</span> · 释放能量</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reaction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reaction</a></span> · 反应</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catalysis&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">catalysis</a></span> · 催化作用</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">By controlling this enzyme, a natural catalyst, scientists changed the rate of gas release and measured the resulting <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emission&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>emission</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">科学家通过控制这种作为天然催化剂的酶改变气体释放速率，并测量由此产生的排放。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-095">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-095" class="vocabulary-word-title">absorb<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-095" aria-label="定位 absorb">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-095" data-mark-label="absorb" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="absorb：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="absorb：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=absorb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 absorb" title="在有道查询 absorb">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-095</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.理解；掌握</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Roots <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=absorb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>absorb</strong></a> each nutrient through uptake mechanisms whose demand may exceed the supply maintained by water circulation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">根通过吸收机制吸收每一种养分，其需求可能超过水循环维持的供应量。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If nutrient uptake rates exceed replenishment, roots cannot <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=absorb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>absorb</strong></a> enough despite continuous water circulation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若养分吸收速率超过补充速率，即使水持续循环，根也无法充分吸收。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Improved water circulation helps roots <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=absorb&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>absorb</strong></a> a nutrient, but uptake cannot exceed the plant's physiological capacity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改善水循环有助于根吸收某种养分，但吸收量不能超过植物的生理能力。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">absorb water</span> · 吸收水分</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transport&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">transport</a></span> · 运输</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=root%20hair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">root hair</a></span> · 根毛</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-096">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-096" class="vocabulary-word-title">circulation<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-096" aria-label="定位 circulation">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-096" data-mark-label="circulation" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="circulation：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="circulation：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=circulation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 circulation" title="在有道查询 circulation">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-096</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 流通；循环；流传</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Roots absorb each nutrient through uptake mechanisms whose demand may exceed the supply maintained by water <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=circulation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>circulation</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">根通过吸收机制吸收每一种养分，其需求可能超过水循环维持的供应量。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If nutrient uptake rates exceed replenishment, roots cannot absorb enough despite continuous water <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=circulation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>circulation</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若养分吸收速率超过补充速率，即使水持续循环，根也无法充分吸收。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Improved water <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=circulation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>circulation</strong></a> helps roots absorb a nutrient, but uptake cannot exceed the plant's physiological capacity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">改善水循环有助于根吸收某种养分，但吸收量不能超过植物的生理能力。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">water circulation</span> · 水循环</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=transport&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">transport</a></span> · 运输</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=root%20hair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">root hair</a></span> · 根毛</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A plant's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=energy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>energy</strong></a> budget depends on its surroundings and its mechanism for capturing light.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">植物的能量收支取决于其生长环境和捕光机制。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers studied the mechanism that saves <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=energy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>energy</strong></a> when a plant's surroundings become dry.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员研究了植物周围环境变干时节省能量的机制。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In shaded surroundings, an efficient light-capturing mechanism helps seedlings obtain enough <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=energy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>energy</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在阴暗环境中，高效的捕光机制帮助幼苗获取足够能量。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">solar energy</span> · 太阳能</p>

### 词群 02.03｜遗传、变异与演化 {#group-02-03}

**串联方式：** 从遗传特征到变异与多样性，再看杂交、繁殖、演化及数量波动；分类是研究工具。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-013">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-013" class="vocabulary-word-title">genetics<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-013" aria-label="定位 genetics">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-013" data-mark-label="genetics" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="genetics：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="genetics：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=genetics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 genetics" title="在有道查询 genetics">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-013</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 遗传学</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=genetics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Genetics</strong></a> explains how a mutation creates variation that can contribute to diversity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">遗传学解释了突变如何产生可能促进多样性的变异。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=genetics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>genetics</strong></a> team linked unusual variation to a mutation rather than to greater species diversity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">遗传学团队将异常变异与一个突变联系起来，而非更高的物种多样性。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=genetics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>genetics</strong></a> studied mutation and variation before measuring diversity in wild populations.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">遗传学学生先研究突变与变异，再测量野生种群的多样性。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-017">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-017" class="vocabulary-word-title">hybridisation<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-017" aria-label="定位 hybridisation">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-017" data-mark-label="hybridisation" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hybridisation：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hybridisation：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hybridisation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hybridisation" title="在有道查询 hybridisation">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-017</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 杂交(= hybridization)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As conditions fluctuate, plants reproduce and evolve, while <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hybridisation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hybridisation</strong></a> can make them harder to classify.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">随着条件波动，植物繁殖并演化，而杂交可能增加分类难度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers classify plants that reproduce after <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hybridisation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hybridisation</strong></a> and examine how they evolve when temperatures fluctuate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员对杂交后繁殖的植物进行分类，并考察它们在温度波动时如何演化。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When populations fluctuate, the plants that reproduce successfully may evolve differently, making <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hybridisation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hybridisation</strong></a> outcomes difficult to classify.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">种群波动时，成功繁殖的植物可能沿不同方向演化，使杂交结果难以分类。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">plant hybridisation</span> · 植物杂交</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adaptation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adaptation</a></span> · 适应</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=population&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">population</a></span> · 种群</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 分类</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As conditions fluctuate, plants reproduce and evolve, while hybridisation can make them harder to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=classify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>classify</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">随着条件波动，植物繁殖并演化，而杂交可能增加分类难度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=classify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>classify</strong></a> plants that reproduce after hybridisation and examine how they evolve when temperatures fluctuate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员对杂交后繁殖的植物进行分类，并考察它们在温度波动时如何演化。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When populations fluctuate, the plants that reproduce successfully may evolve differently, making hybridisation outcomes difficult to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=classify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>classify</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">种群波动时，成功繁殖的植物可能沿不同方向演化，使杂交结果难以分类。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">classify species</span> · 分类物种</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adaptation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adaptation</a></span> · 适应</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=population&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">population</a></span> · 种群</li></ul>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As conditions fluctuate, plants <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reproduce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reproduce</strong></a> and evolve, while hybridisation can make them harder to classify.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">随着条件波动，植物繁殖并演化，而杂交可能增加分类难度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers classify plants that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reproduce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reproduce</strong></a> after hybridisation and examine how they evolve when temperatures fluctuate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员对杂交后繁殖的植物进行分类，并考察它们在温度波动时如何演化。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When populations fluctuate, the plants that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reproduce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reproduce</strong></a> successfully may evolve differently, making hybridisation outcomes difficult to classify.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">种群波动时，成功繁殖的植物可能沿不同方向演化，使杂交结果难以分类。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-020">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 进化；(使)发展，(使)逐步形成</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As conditions fluctuate, plants reproduce and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evolve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>evolve</strong></a>, while hybridisation can make them harder to classify.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">随着条件波动，植物繁殖并演化，而杂交可能增加分类难度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers classify plants that reproduce after hybridisation and examine how they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evolve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>evolve</strong></a> when temperatures fluctuate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员对杂交后繁殖的植物进行分类，并考察它们在温度波动时如何演化。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When populations fluctuate, the plants that reproduce successfully may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evolve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>evolve</strong></a> differently, making hybridisation outcomes difficult to classify.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">种群波动时，成功繁殖的植物可能沿不同方向演化，使杂交结果难以分类。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">evolve over time</span> · 随时间演化</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adaptation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adaptation</a></span> · 适应</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=population&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">population</a></span> · 种群</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-021">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-021" class="vocabulary-word-title">fluctuate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-021" aria-label="定位 fluctuate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-021" data-mark-label="fluctuate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fluctuate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fluctuate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fluctuate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fluctuate" title="在有道查询 fluctuate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-021</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 波动，起伏</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As conditions <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fluctuate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fluctuate</strong></a>, plants reproduce and evolve, while hybridisation can make them harder to classify.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">随着条件波动，植物繁殖并演化，而杂交可能增加分类难度。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers classify plants that reproduce after hybridisation and examine how they evolve when temperatures <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fluctuate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fluctuate</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员对杂交后繁殖的植物进行分类，并考察它们在温度波动时如何演化。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When populations <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fluctuate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fluctuate</strong></a>, the plants that reproduce successfully may evolve differently, making hybridisation outcomes difficult to classify.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">种群波动时，成功繁殖的植物可能沿不同方向演化，使杂交结果难以分类。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">fluctuate seasonally</span> · 季节性波动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adaptation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adaptation</a></span> · 适应</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=population&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">population</a></span> · 种群</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 02.04｜种子到果实 {#group-02-04}

**串联方式：** 用花粉、萌发、嫩芽、开花、香气和成熟果实重建一株植物的生长过程。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-044">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-044" class="vocabulary-word-title">pollen<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-044" aria-label="定位 pollen">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-044" data-mark-label="pollen" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pollen：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pollen：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pollen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pollen" title="在有道查询 pollen">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-044</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pollen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pollen</strong></a> fertilises the ovule, a seed develops and may later germinate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">花粉使胚珠受精后，种子发育形成，之后可能萌发。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The class followed <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pollen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pollen</strong></a> from a flower to the formation of a seed that could germinate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生追踪花粉从花朵到形成能够萌发的种子的过程。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Without compatible <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pollen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pollen</strong></a>, this plant cannot produce a viable seed to germinate next spring.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">没有相容花粉，这种植物无法产生能在来年春天萌发的活种子。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pollination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">pollination</a></span> · 授粉</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seedling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">seedling</a></span> · 幼苗</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-047">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As new shoots <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=burgeon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>burgeon</strong></a>, each bud gradually opens into a flower.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新枝蓬勃生长时，每个花蕾逐渐绽放成花。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Warm weather helps the garden <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=burgeon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>burgeon</strong></a> as bud after bud becomes a flower.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">温暖天气使花园蓬勃生长，一个接一个花蕾绽放成花。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We watched the plants <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=burgeon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>burgeon</strong></a>, recording when each bud produced its first flower.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们观察植物茁壮生长，记录每个花蕾何时开出第一朵花。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Warm weather helps the garden burgeon as <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bud&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bud</strong></a> after <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bud&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bud</strong></a> becomes a flower.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">温暖天气使花园蓬勃生长，一个接一个花蕾绽放成花。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As new shoots burgeon, each bud gradually opens into a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flower&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flower</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新枝蓬勃生长时，每个花蕾逐渐绽放成花。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Warm weather helps the garden burgeon as bud after bud becomes a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flower&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flower</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">温暖天气使花园蓬勃生长，一个接一个花蕾绽放成花。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We watched the plants burgeon, recording when each bud produced its first <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flower&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flower</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们观察植物茁壮生长，记录每个花蕾何时开出第一朵花。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a white flower</span> · 白花</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shoot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">shoot</a></span> · 嫩芽</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=petal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">petal</a></span> · 花瓣</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-050">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-050" class="vocabulary-word-title">blossom<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-050" aria-label="定位 blossom">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-050" data-mark-label="blossom" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="blossom：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="blossom：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blossom&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 blossom" title="在有道查询 blossom">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-050</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 开 花；n. 花 朵</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Cherry <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blossom&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blossom</strong></a> filled the garden with scent while aromatic herbs began to bloom.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">樱花令花园充满香气，芳香草本也开始开花。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In full bloom, the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blossom&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blossom</strong></a> had a delicate scent beside the stronger aromatic lavender.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">盛开时，花朵散发淡雅香气，旁边薰衣草的芳香更浓。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors enjoyed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=blossom&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>blossom</strong></a> and its scent as the aromatic borders came into bloom.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">芳香的花坛进入花期时，游客欣赏花朵及其香气。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 02.05｜枯萎与分解 {#group-02-05}

**串联方式：** 植物枯萎后，联系真菌、霉菌与腐烂分解；stale 还可描述食物不新鲜。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-042">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-042" class="vocabulary-word-title">fungus<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-042" aria-label="定位 fungus">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-042" data-mark-label="fungus" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fungus：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fungus：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fungus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fungus" title="在有道查询 fungus">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-042</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 真菌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The mould covering the bread is a type of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fungus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fungus</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">覆盖面包的霉菌是一种真菌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A damp cellar encourages mould and other <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fungus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fungus</strong></a> growth.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">潮湿地窖会促进霉菌及其他真菌生长。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fungus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fungus</strong></a> releases spores, as does the mould found on stored fruit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种真菌会释放孢子，储藏水果上的霉菌也是如此。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an edible fungus</span> · 食用菌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">spore</a></span> · 孢子</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dampness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dampness</a></span> · 潮湿</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-043">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-043" class="vocabulary-word-title">mould<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-043" aria-label="定位 mould">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-043" data-mark-label="mould" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mould：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mould：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mould&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mould" title="在有道查询 mould">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-043</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">(= mold)；n. 霉 菌；v. 发 霉</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mould&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mould</strong></a> covering the bread is a type of fungus.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">覆盖面包的霉菌是一种真菌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A damp cellar encourages <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mould&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mould</strong></a> and other fungus growth.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">潮湿地窖会促进霉菌及其他真菌生长。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">This fungus releases spores, as does the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mould&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mould</strong></a> found on stored fruit.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种真菌会释放孢子，储藏水果上的霉菌也是如此。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">black mould</span> · 黑霉</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">spore</a></span> · 孢子</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dampness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dampness</a></span> · 潮湿</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-056">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-056" class="vocabulary-word-title">wither<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-056" aria-label="定位 wither">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-056" data-mark-label="wither" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="wither：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="wither：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wither&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 wither" title="在有道查询 wither">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-056</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.(使)枯萎</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Leaves <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wither&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wither</strong></a>, stale bread grows mould, and both eventually decompose through rot and decay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">叶片枯萎，陈面包长霉，两者最终都通过腐烂和腐解而分解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The compost contains plants that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wither&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wither</strong></a> in autumn and stale food left to decompose through rot and decay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">堆肥含有秋天枯萎的植物和留待通过腐烂、腐解而分解的不新鲜食物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When plants <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wither&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wither</strong></a>, their tissues decompose; like stale food, they undergo rot and decay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">植物枯萎后，组织会分解；它们像不新鲜的食物一样经历腐烂和腐解。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">wither in heat</span> · 在高温中枯萎</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decomposition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">decomposition</a></span> · 分解</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compost&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compost</a></span> · 堆肥</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-057">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-057" class="vocabulary-word-title">decompose<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-057" aria-label="定位 decompose">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-057" data-mark-label="decompose" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="decompose：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="decompose：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decompose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 decompose" title="在有道查询 decompose">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-057</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.(使)分解；(使)腐烂</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Leaves wither, stale bread grows mould, and both eventually <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decompose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>decompose</strong></a> through rot and decay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">叶片枯萎，陈面包长霉，两者最终都通过腐烂和腐解而分解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The compost contains plants that wither in autumn and stale food left to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decompose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>decompose</strong></a> through rot and decay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">堆肥含有秋天枯萎的植物和留待通过腐烂、腐解而分解的不新鲜食物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When plants wither, their tissues <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decompose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>decompose</strong></a>; like stale food, they undergo rot and decay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">植物枯萎后，组织会分解；它们像不新鲜的食物一样经历腐烂和腐解。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">decompose naturally</span> · 自然分解</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decomposition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">decomposition</a></span> · 分解</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compost&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compost</a></span> · 堆肥</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-058">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-058" class="vocabulary-word-title">rot<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-058" aria-label="定位 rot">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-058" data-mark-label="rot" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rot：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rot：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rot" title="在有道查询 rot">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-058</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.(使)腐烂，(使)腐坏；n. 腐烂</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Leaves wither, stale bread grows mould, and both eventually decompose through <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rot</strong></a> and decay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">叶片枯萎，陈面包长霉，两者最终都通过腐烂和腐解而分解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The compost contains plants that wither in autumn and stale food left to decompose through <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rot</strong></a> and decay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">堆肥含有秋天枯萎的植物和留待通过腐烂、腐解而分解的不新鲜食物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When plants wither, their tissues decompose; like stale food, they undergo <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rot&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rot</strong></a> and decay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">植物枯萎后，组织会分解；它们像不新鲜的食物一样经历腐烂和腐解。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">root rot</span> · 根腐病</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decomposition&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">decomposition</a></span> · 分解</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=compost&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">compost</a></span> · 堆肥</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-059">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Leaves wither, stale bread grows mould, and both eventually decompose through rot and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>decay</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">叶片枯萎，陈面包长霉，两者最终都通过腐烂和腐解而分解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The compost contains plants that wither in autumn and stale food left to decompose through rot and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>decay</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">堆肥含有秋天枯萎的植物和留待通过腐烂、腐解而分解的不新鲜食物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When plants wither, their tissues decompose; like stale food, they undergo rot and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>decay</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">植物枯萎后，组织会分解；它们像不新鲜的食物一样经历腐烂和腐解。</p></li>
</ol>
