<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The compost contains plants that wither in autumn and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stale</strong></a> food left to decompose through rot and decay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">堆肥含有秋天枯萎的植物和留待通过腐烂、腐解而分解的不新鲜食物。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When plants wither, their tissues decompose; like <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stale</strong></a> food, they undergo rot and decay.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">植物枯萎后，组织会分解；它们像不新鲜的食物一样经历腐烂和腐解。</p></li>

### 词群 02.06｜从整地到收获 {#group-02-06}

**串联方式：** 开垦、耕作、播种，再采摘收获并核算产量；rear 常用于饲养动物，作为农业延伸。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-022">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-022" class="vocabulary-word-title">reclaim<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-022" aria-label="定位 reclaim">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-022" data-mark-label="reclaim" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reclaim：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reclaim：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reclaim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reclaim" title="在有道查询 reclaim">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-022</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 开垦，开拓，利用</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Farmers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reclaim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reclaim</strong></a> unused land, cultivate the soil, sow wheat and harvest it in summer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民开垦闲置土地、耕作土壤、播种小麦，并在夏季收割。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they sow or harvest crops, they must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reclaim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reclaim</strong></a> the plot and cultivate its compacted soil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">播种或收获作物前，他们必须开垦地块并耕松板结土壤。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The project helps families <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reclaim&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reclaim</strong></a> fields, cultivate vegetables, sow suitable varieties and harvest fresh food.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">项目帮助家庭开垦田地、种植蔬菜、播种合适品种并收获新鲜食物。</p></li>
</ol>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 采，摘</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Families rear goats, pick apples and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pluck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pluck</strong></a> tea leaves to supplement the rice yield.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">家庭通过养山羊、采苹果和摘茶叶来补充稻米产量带来的收入。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pluck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pluck</strong></a> young leaves and pick ripe berries, while neighbours rear animals and record their crop yield.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人摘嫩叶、采熟浆果，邻居则饲养动物并记录作物产量。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farm's yield supports families who rear poultry, pick fruit and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pluck&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pluck</strong></a> herbs for market.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农场的收成供养着养家禽、摘水果并采草药出售的家庭。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">pluck a leaf</span> · 摘下一片叶子</p>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Families <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rear</strong></a> goats, pick apples and pluck tea leaves to supplement the rice yield.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">家庭通过养山羊、采苹果和摘茶叶来补充稻米产量带来的收入。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers pluck young leaves and pick ripe berries, while neighbours <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rear</strong></a> animals and record their crop yield.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人摘嫩叶、采熟浆果，邻居则饲养动物并记录作物产量。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farm's yield supports families who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rear</strong></a> poultry, pick fruit and pluck herbs for market.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农场的收成供养着养家禽、摘水果并采草药出售的家庭。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">rear livestock</span> · 饲养牲畜</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=husbandry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">husbandry</a></span> · 畜牧业</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=harvesting&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">harvesting</a></span> · 采收</li></ul>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-030">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">On this <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arable</strong></a> plot, we plough large areas and use a spade and rake around the edges.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在这片耕地上，我们犁耕大片区域，并用铲子和耙子处理边缘。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A farmer may plough <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arable</strong></a> land mechanically but still need a spade and rake for smaller beds.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民可能机械犁耕耕地，但处理小菜畦仍需铲子和耙子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Bring a spade and rake after we plough the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arable</strong></a> field.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们犁完这块耕地后，请带来铲子和耙子。</p></li>
</ol>

</div>
</details>

### 词群 02.07｜收获后怎样整理 {#group-02-07}

**串联方式：** 把植物材料堆起、捆起或扎成束，再把花放进花瓶；区别成堆与成束。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-034">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-034" class="vocabulary-word-title">stack<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-034" aria-label="定位 stack">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-034" data-mark-label="stack" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="stack：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="stack：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 stack" title="在有道查询 stack">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-034</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 堆，垛；v. 堆积</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A vase held a bunch of flowers beside a bundle of straw, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stack</strong></a> of hay and a heap of leaves.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">花瓶里插着一束花，旁边是一捆稻草、一垛干草和一堆树叶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She took a bundle from the straw <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stack</strong></a>, cleared a heap of leaves and placed a bunch of flowers in a vase.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她从草垛取下一捆稻草，清走一堆树叶，并在花瓶里插上一束花。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For the display, put the bunch in a vase and place the bundle beside the hay <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stack&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stack</strong></a> and leaf heap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">布展时，将花束插入花瓶，把草捆放在草垛和树叶堆旁。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a stack of hay</span> · 一垛干草</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=storage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">storage</a></span> · 储藏</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrangement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">arrangement</a></span> · 摆放</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-035">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A vase held a bunch of flowers beside a bundle of straw, a stack of hay and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>heap</strong></a> of leaves.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">花瓶里插着一束花，旁边是一捆稻草、一垛干草和一堆树叶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She took a bundle from the straw stack, cleared a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>heap</strong></a> of leaves and placed a bunch of flowers in a vase.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她从草垛取下一捆稻草，清走一堆树叶，并在花瓶里插上一束花。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For the display, put the bunch in a vase and place the bundle beside the hay stack and leaf <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>heap</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">布展时，将花束插入花瓶，把草捆放在草垛和树叶堆旁。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A vase held a bunch of flowers beside a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bundle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bundle</strong></a> of straw, a stack of hay and a heap of leaves.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">花瓶里插着一束花，旁边是一捆稻草、一垛干草和一堆树叶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She took a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bundle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bundle</strong></a> from the straw stack, cleared a heap of leaves and placed a bunch of flowers in a vase.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她从草垛取下一捆稻草，清走一堆树叶，并在花瓶里插上一束花。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For the display, put the bunch in a vase and place the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bundle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bundle</strong></a> beside the hay stack and leaf heap.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">布展时，将花束插入花瓶，把草捆放在草垛和树叶堆旁。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a bundle of straw</span> · 一捆稻草</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=storage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">storage</a></span> · 储藏</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrangement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">arrangement</a></span> · 摆放</li></ul>
</div>

### 词群 02.08｜从森林到林业产品 {#group-02-08}

**串联方式：** 比较雨林、丛林和人工种植地，再连接林业、木材、原木与木炭。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-061">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-061" class="vocabulary-word-title">rainforest<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-061" aria-label="定位 rainforest">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-061" data-mark-label="rainforest" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rainforest：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rainforest：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rainforest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rainforest" title="在有道查询 rainforest">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-061</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 雨林</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Beyond the field, a tea plantation climbed the terrace towards dense jungle and intact <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rainforest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rainforest</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">越过田地，一片茶园沿梯田向茂密丛林和完整雨林延伸。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The map distinguishes <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rainforest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rainforest</strong></a> and jungle from a plantation, a cultivated field and a mountain terrace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地图区分雨林和丛林，与种植园、农田及山地梯田。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Converting <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rainforest&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rainforest</strong></a> or jungle into a plantation, field or terrace can change local water cycles.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">将雨林或丛林变成种植园、农田或梯田可能改变当地水循环。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">tropical rainforest</span> · 热带雨林</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=canopy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">canopy</a></span> · 树冠层</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=land%20use&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">land use</a></span> · 土地利用</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-062">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The map distinguishes rainforest and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jungle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jungle</strong></a> from a plantation, a cultivated field and a mountain terrace.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地图区分雨林和丛林，与种植园、农田及山地梯田。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Converting rainforest or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=jungle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>jungle</strong></a> into a plantation, field or terrace can change local water cycles.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">将雨林或丛林变成种植园、农田或梯田可能改变当地水循环。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Beyond the field, a tea plantation climbed the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terrace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>terrace</strong></a> towards dense jungle and intact rainforest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">越过田地，一片茶园沿梯田向茂密丛林和完整雨林延伸。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The map distinguishes rainforest and jungle from a plantation, a cultivated field and a mountain <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terrace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>terrace</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地图区分雨林和丛林，与种植园、农田及山地梯田。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Converting rainforest or jungle into a plantation, field or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terrace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>terrace</strong></a> can change local water cycles.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">将雨林或丛林变成种植园、农田或梯田可能改变当地水循环。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a cultivated terrace</span> · 耕作梯田</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=canopy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">canopy</a></span> · 树冠层</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=land%20use&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">land use</a></span> · 土地利用</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-066">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-066" class="vocabulary-word-title">timber<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-066" aria-label="定位 timber">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-066" data-mark-label="timber" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="timber：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="timber：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=timber&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 timber" title="在有道查询 timber">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-066</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 木材，木料；林木</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Sustainable forestry turns a harvested log into <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=timber&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>timber</strong></a> while using suitable waste for charcoal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">可持续林业把采下的原木加工成木材，并用合适废料制木炭。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The forestry cooperative sells <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=timber&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>timber</strong></a> and charcoal rather than exporting every log unprocessed.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">林业合作社出售木材和木炭，而非将每根原木未经加工出口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A forestry student traced one log from the forest to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=timber&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>timber</strong></a> production and charcoal manufacture.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一名林业学生追踪一根原木从森林到木材加工和木炭制造的过程。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">sustainable timber</span> · 可持续来源木材</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=logging&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">logging</a></span> · 采伐</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sawmill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sawmill</a></span> · 锯木厂</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-067">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-067" class="vocabulary-word-title">charcoal<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-067" aria-label="定位 charcoal">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-067" data-mark-label="charcoal" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="charcoal：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="charcoal：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=charcoal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 charcoal" title="在有道查询 charcoal">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-067</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 木炭</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Sustainable forestry turns a harvested log into timber while using suitable waste for <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=charcoal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>charcoal</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">可持续林业把采下的原木加工成木材，并用合适废料制木炭。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The forestry cooperative sells timber and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=charcoal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>charcoal</strong></a> rather than exporting every log unprocessed.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">林业合作社出售木材和木炭，而非将每根原木未经加工出口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A forestry student traced one log from the forest to timber production and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=charcoal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>charcoal</strong></a> manufacture.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一名林业学生追踪一根原木从森林到木材加工和木炭制造的过程。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">wood charcoal</span> · 木炭</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=logging&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">logging</a></span> · 采伐</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sawmill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sawmill</a></span> · 锯木厂</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-068">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-068" class="vocabulary-word-title">log<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-068" aria-label="定位 log">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-068" data-mark-label="log" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="log：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="log：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=log&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 log" title="在有道查询 log">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-068</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 原木；(航海、飞行)日志</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Sustainable forestry turns a harvested <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=log&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>log</strong></a> into timber while using suitable waste for charcoal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">可持续林业把采下的原木加工成木材，并用合适废料制木炭。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The forestry cooperative sells timber and charcoal rather than exporting every <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=log&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>log</strong></a> unprocessed.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">林业合作社出售木材和木炭，而非将每根原木未经加工出口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A forestry student traced one <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=log&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>log</strong></a> from the forest to timber production and charcoal manufacture.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一名林业学生追踪一根原木从森林到木材加工和木炭制造的过程。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a fallen log</span> · 倒下的原木</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=logging&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">logging</a></span> · 采伐</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sawmill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sawmill</a></span> · 锯木厂</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-070">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Sustainable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=forestry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>forestry</strong></a> turns a harvested log into timber while using suitable waste for charcoal.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">可持续林业把采下的原木加工成木材，并用合适废料制木炭。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=forestry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>forestry</strong></a> cooperative sells timber and charcoal rather than exporting every log unprocessed.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">林业合作社出售木材和木炭，而非将每根原木未经加工出口。</p></li>

### 词群 02.09｜近形词辨认：log 与 logo {#group-02-09}

**串联方式：** logo 是标志，不是植物部位；保留这一词条，用与 log 的词形对照帮助避免误记。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-069">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-069" class="vocabulary-word-title">logo<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-069" aria-label="定位 logo">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-069" data-mark-label="logo" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="logo：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="logo：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=logo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 logo" title="在有道查询 logo">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-069</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 标识，标志，徽标</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The company printed its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=logo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>logo</strong></a> on recycled packaging.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公司将标志印在再生包装上。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A simple <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=logo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>logo</strong></a> helps customers recognise the brand.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">简洁的标志帮助顾客识别品牌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=logo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>logo</strong></a> features a green leaf.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新标志包含一片绿叶。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a company logo</span> · 公司标志</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=brand&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">brand</a></span> · 品牌</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">近义</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=emblem&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">emblem</a></span> · 徽记</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 02.10｜植物部位与地面植被 {#group-02-10}

**串联方式：** 从树干、树枝到根，再看草、芦苇、荆棘、杂草和草地。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-071">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-071" class="vocabulary-word-title">branch<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-071" aria-label="定位 branch">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-071" data-mark-label="branch" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="branch：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="branch：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=branch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 branch" title="在有道查询 branch">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-071</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 树枝；(企业或组织的)分支机构，分店</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The heavy bough split from the trunk, leaving a smaller <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=branch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>branch</strong></a> hanging above an exposed root.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">粗大的树枝从树干裂开，留下一根较小树枝悬在裸露树根上方。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We measured the trunk, sampled a root and compared a young <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=branch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>branch</strong></a> with an old bough.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们测量树干、采集树根样本，并比较嫩枝与老的大枝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A strong root anchored the trunk while snow weighed down each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=branch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>branch</strong></a> and bough.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">牢固的树根固定着树干，积雪则压在每根大小树枝上。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a tree branch</span> · 树枝</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bark</a></span> · 树皮</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=twig&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">twig</a></span> · 细枝</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-072">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-072" class="vocabulary-word-title">trunk<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-072" aria-label="定位 trunk">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-072" data-mark-label="trunk" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="trunk：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="trunk：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trunk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 trunk" title="在有道查询 trunk">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-072</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 树干；其他语境可指象鼻、行李箱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The heavy bough split from the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trunk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trunk</strong></a>, leaving a smaller branch hanging above an exposed root.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">粗大的树枝从树干裂开，留下一根较小树枝悬在裸露树根上方。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We measured the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trunk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trunk</strong></a>, sampled a root and compared a young branch with an old bough.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们测量树干、采集树根样本，并比较嫩枝与老的大枝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A strong root anchored the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trunk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trunk</strong></a> while snow weighed down each branch and bough.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">牢固的树根固定着树干，积雪则压在每根大小树枝上。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a thick trunk</span> · 粗壮树干</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bark&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bark</a></span> · 树皮</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=twig&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">twig</a></span> · 细枝</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-075">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 干草</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The farmer stored <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hay</strong></a> and straw beside a reed mat, taking care to remove a sharp thorn.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">农民把干草和麦秆存放在芦苇席旁，小心取出一根尖刺。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A thorn caught my sleeve as I carried <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hay</strong></a> past a straw roof and a reed fence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我搬着干草经过麦秆屋顶和芦苇篱笆时，一根尖刺勾住了袖子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The nest contained <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hay</strong></a>, straw and reed fibres, with a thorn lodged in its outer wall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">巢里有干草、麦秆和芦苇纤维，外壁还卡着一根尖刺。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">hay for cattle</span> · 喂牛的干草</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fodder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fodder</a></span> · 饲料</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=thatch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">thatch</a></span> · 茅草屋顶材料</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-079">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-079" class="vocabulary-word-title">weed<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-079" aria-label="定位 weed">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-079" data-mark-label="weed" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="weed：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="weed：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=weed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 weed" title="在有道查询 weed">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-079</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 杂草，野草；v. 除杂草</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A plant dismissed as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=weed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>weed</strong></a> on a lawn may belong naturally among grass in a meadow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">在草坪上被当作杂草的植物，可能天然属于草甸中的草本群落。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike a closely cut lawn, the meadow supports tall grass and flowers some gardeners call a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=weed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>weed</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与修剪整齐的草坪不同，草甸生长着高草和一些被园丁称为杂草的花。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We removed an invasive <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=weed&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>weed</strong></a> from the meadow but left native grass beside the lawn.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们从草甸清除一种入侵杂草，但保留了草坪旁的本土草类。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">remove a weed</span> · 清除杂草</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=turf&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">turf</a></span> · 草皮</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wildflower&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wildflower</a></span> · 野花</li></ul>

</details>

### 词群 02.11｜给具体植物与水域对象命名 {#group-02-11}

**串联方式：** 先认树木、藤本、花卉与香草，再转入珊瑚礁和藻类；reef 是栖息环境，不是植物。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-083">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-083" class="vocabulary-word-title">olive<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-083" aria-label="定位 olive">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-083" data-mark-label="olive" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="olive：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="olive：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=olive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 olive" title="在有道查询 olive">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-083</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 橄榄；橄榄树</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=olive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>olive</strong></a> tree and a grape vine grew below the pine-covered hill.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一棵橄榄树和一株葡萄藤生长在长满松树的山坡下。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The garden has a pine beside an old <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=olive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>olive</strong></a> tree and a vine trained along the wall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">花园里，一棵松树长在老橄榄树旁，一株藤蔓沿墙攀爬。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Drought damaged the vine and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=olive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>olive</strong></a> tree, while the mature pine survived.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">干旱损伤了葡萄藤和橄榄树，而成年的松树存活了下来。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an olive tree</span> · 橄榄树</p>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An olive tree and a grape vine grew below the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pine</strong></a>-covered hill.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一棵橄榄树和一株葡萄藤生长在长满松树的山坡下。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The garden has a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pine</strong></a> beside an old olive tree and a vine trained along the wall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">花园里，一棵松树长在老橄榄树旁，一株藤蔓沿墙攀爬。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Drought damaged the vine and olive tree, while the mature <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pine</strong></a> survived.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">干旱损伤了葡萄藤和橄榄树，而成年的松树存活了下来。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=violet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>violet</strong></a> grew beside a red tulip and a pot of mint.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一株紫罗兰长在一株红郁金香和一盆薄荷旁。</p></li>
