### 词群 02.12｜生态受损与保护 {#group-02-12}

**串联方式：** 从平衡和保护谈起，再描述林火、毁坏和灭绝风险；infringe 可接到侵害保护规定。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-103">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-103" class="vocabulary-word-title">counterbalance<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-103" aria-label="定位 counterbalance">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-103" data-mark-label="counterbalance" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="counterbalance：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="counterbalance：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=counterbalance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 counterbalance" title="在有道查询 counterbalance">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-103</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.起平衡作用的事物；抗衡力；v.抵消；对……起平衡作用</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Conservation projects protect species and preserve habitats, but may not fully <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=counterbalance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>counterbalance</strong></a> losses elsewhere.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">自然保护项目保护物种并保存栖息地，但可能无法完全抵消别处的损失。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=counterbalance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>counterbalance</strong></a> development pressures, conservation funds help protect wetlands and preserve ancient woodland.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为抵消开发压力，自然保护资金帮助保护湿地并保存古老林地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Successful conservation can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=counterbalance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>counterbalance</strong></a> past damage if policies protect whole ecosystems and preserve their diversity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若政策保护完整生态系统并保存其多样性，成功的自然保护便能抵消以往损害。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">counterbalance losses</span> · 抵消损失</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restoration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">restoration</a></span> · 修复</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protection</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-104">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-104" class="vocabulary-word-title">protect<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-104" aria-label="定位 protect">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-104" data-mark-label="protect" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="protect：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="protect：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 protect" title="在有道查询 protect">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-104</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 保护</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Conservation projects <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>protect</strong></a> species and preserve habitats, but may not fully counterbalance losses elsewhere.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">自然保护项目保护物种并保存栖息地，但可能无法完全抵消别处的损失。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To counterbalance development pressures, conservation funds help <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>protect</strong></a> wetlands and preserve ancient woodland.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为抵消开发压力，自然保护资金帮助保护湿地并保存古老林地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Successful conservation can counterbalance past damage if policies <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>protect</strong></a> whole ecosystems and preserve their diversity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若政策保护完整生态系统并保存其多样性，成功的自然保护便能抵消以往损害。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">protect wildlife</span> · 保护野生生物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restoration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">restoration</a></span> · 修复</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protection</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-105">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-105" class="vocabulary-word-title">preserve<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-105" aria-label="定位 preserve">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-105" data-mark-label="preserve" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="preserve：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="preserve：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preserve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 preserve" title="在有道查询 preserve">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-105</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 保护；维持(原状)；保存</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Conservation projects protect species and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preserve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>preserve</strong></a> habitats, but may not fully counterbalance losses elsewhere.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">自然保护项目保护物种并保存栖息地，但可能无法完全抵消别处的损失。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To counterbalance development pressures, conservation funds help protect wetlands and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preserve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>preserve</strong></a> ancient woodland.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为抵消开发压力，自然保护资金帮助保护湿地并保存古老林地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Successful conservation can counterbalance past damage if policies protect whole ecosystems and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preserve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>preserve</strong></a> their diversity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若政策保护完整生态系统并保存其多样性，成功的自然保护便能抵消以往损害。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">preserve habitats</span> · 保存栖息地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restoration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">restoration</a></span> · 修复</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protection</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-106">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-106" class="vocabulary-word-title">conservation<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-106" aria-label="定位 conservation">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-106" data-mark-label="conservation" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="conservation：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="conservation：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conservation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 conservation" title="在有道查询 conservation">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-106</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.(对自然环境的)保护；保存</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conservation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Conservation</strong></a> projects protect species and preserve habitats, but may not fully counterbalance losses elsewhere.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">自然保护项目保护物种并保存栖息地，但可能无法完全抵消别处的损失。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To counterbalance development pressures, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conservation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>conservation</strong></a> funds help protect wetlands and preserve ancient woodland.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为抵消开发压力，自然保护资金帮助保护湿地并保存古老林地。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Successful <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conservation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>conservation</strong></a> can counterbalance past damage if policies protect whole ecosystems and preserve their diversity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若政策保护完整生态系统并保存其多样性，成功的自然保护便能抵消以往损害。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">nature conservation</span> · 自然保护</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restoration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">restoration</a></span> · 修复</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protection</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-107">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-107" class="vocabulary-word-title">bush fire<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-107" aria-label="定位 bush fire">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-107" data-mark-label="bush fire" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bush fire：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bush fire：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bush%20fire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bush fire" title="在有道查询 bush fire">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-107</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 林区大火</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Crews worked through the night to extinguish the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bush%20fire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bush fire</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">消防队连夜奋战扑灭灌木林火。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Heavy rain helped firefighters extinguish the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bush%20fire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bush fire</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大雨帮助消防员扑灭灌木林火。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Aircraft dropped water to extinguish the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bush%20fire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bush fire</strong></a> near the village.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">飞机洒水扑灭村庄附近的灌木林火。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an uncontrolled bush fire</span> · 失控的灌木林火</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=firebreak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">firebreak</a></span> · 防火隔离带</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=firefighter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">firefighter</a></span> · 消防员</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-108">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-108" class="vocabulary-word-title">extinguish<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-108" aria-label="定位 extinguish">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-108" data-mark-label="extinguish" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="extinguish：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="extinguish：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=extinguish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 extinguish" title="在有道查询 extinguish">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-108</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 扑灭(火)；使(想法、感情等)破灭</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Crews worked through the night to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=extinguish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>extinguish</strong></a> the bush fire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">消防队连夜奋战扑灭灌木林火。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Heavy rain helped firefighters <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=extinguish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>extinguish</strong></a> the bush fire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大雨帮助消防员扑灭灌木林火。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Aircraft dropped water to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=extinguish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>extinguish</strong></a> the bush fire near the village.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">飞机洒水扑灭村庄附近的灌木林火。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">extinguish a fire</span> · 扑灭火灾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=firebreak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">firebreak</a></span> · 防火隔离带</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=firefighter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">firefighter</a></span> · 消防员</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-109">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-109" class="vocabulary-word-title">destruct<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-109" aria-label="定位 destruct">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-109" data-mark-label="destruct" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="destruct：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="destruct：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destruct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 destruct" title="在有道查询 destruct">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-109</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 毁坏；自毁；一般表达“破坏”更常用 destroy</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The damaged satellite was programmed to self-<a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destruct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>destruct</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这颗损坏的卫星被设定为自毁。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The device will self-<a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destruct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>destruct</strong></a> if someone opens its sealed casing.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果有人打开密封外壳，这个装置就会自毁。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Engineers disabled the mechanism that could make the prototype self-<a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destruct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>destruct</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工程师关闭了可能使原型自毁的机制。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">self-destruct</span> · 自毁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">词形</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destruction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">destruction</a></span> · 破坏</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">近义</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destroy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">destroy</a></span> · 摧毁</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-110">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-110" class="vocabulary-word-title">ruin<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-110" aria-label="定位 ruin">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-110" data-mark-label="ruin" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="ruin：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="ruin：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ruin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 ruin" title="在有道查询 ruin">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-110</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 毁坏，摧毁；n. 毁灭；废墟</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Plans to demolish the old barn could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ruin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ruin</strong></a> a nesting site and cause young birds to perish.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">拆除旧谷仓的计划可能毁坏筑巢地点，导致幼鸟死亡。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Careless crews can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ruin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ruin</strong></a> habitats when they demolish structures, leaving trapped animals to perish.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">施工队拆除建筑时若疏忽大意，可能毁坏栖息地，令被困动物死亡。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before workers demolish the bridge, ecologists will relocate nests so chicks do not perish and machinery does not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ruin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ruin</strong></a> the riverbank.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人拆桥前，生态学家将迁移鸟巢，避免雏鸟死亡及机械毁坏河岸。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">ruin a habitat</span> · 毁坏栖息地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destruction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">destruction</a></span> · 破坏</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=survival&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">survival</a></span> · 存活</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-111">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-111" class="vocabulary-word-title">perish<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-111" aria-label="定位 perish">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-111" data-mark-label="perish" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="perish：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="perish：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 perish" title="在有道查询 perish">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-111</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 毁灭，消亡；腐烂</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Plans to demolish the old barn could ruin a nesting site and cause young birds to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perish</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">拆除旧谷仓的计划可能毁坏筑巢地点，导致幼鸟死亡。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Careless crews can ruin habitats when they demolish structures, leaving trapped animals to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perish</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">施工队拆除建筑时若疏忽大意，可能毁坏栖息地，令被困动物死亡。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before workers demolish the bridge, ecologists will relocate nests so chicks do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>perish</strong></a> and machinery does not ruin the riverbank.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人拆桥前，生态学家将迁移鸟巢，避免雏鸟死亡及机械毁坏河岸。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">perish in a fire</span> · 在火灾中死亡</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destruction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">destruction</a></span> · 破坏</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=survival&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">survival</a></span> · 存活</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-112">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-112" class="vocabulary-word-title">demolish<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-112" aria-label="定位 demolish">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-112" data-mark-label="demolish" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="demolish：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="demolish：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demolish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 demolish" title="在有道查询 demolish">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-112</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 拆除；毁坏，破坏；推翻</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Plans to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demolish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>demolish</strong></a> the old barn could ruin a nesting site and cause young birds to perish.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">拆除旧谷仓的计划可能毁坏筑巢地点，导致幼鸟死亡。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Careless crews can ruin habitats when they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demolish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>demolish</strong></a> structures, leaving trapped animals to perish.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">施工队拆除建筑时若疏忽大意，可能毁坏栖息地，令被困动物死亡。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before workers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=demolish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>demolish</strong></a> the bridge, ecologists will relocate nests so chicks do not perish and machinery does not ruin the riverbank.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人拆桥前，生态学家将迁移鸟巢，避免雏鸟死亡及机械毁坏河岸。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">demolish a building</span> · 拆除建筑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=destruction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">destruction</a></span> · 破坏</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=survival&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">survival</a></span> · 存活</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-113">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-113" class="vocabulary-word-title">infringe<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-113" aria-label="定位 infringe">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-113" data-mark-label="infringe" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="infringe：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="infringe：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=infringe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 infringe" title="在有道查询 infringe">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-113</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 侵犯；违反</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Illegal logging can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=infringe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>infringe</strong></a> community rights, undermine conservation and accelerate extinction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">非法采伐可能侵犯社区权利、损害保护工作并加速灭绝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Policies that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=infringe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>infringe</strong></a> indigenous rights may undermine cooperation needed to prevent extinction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">侵犯原住民权利的政策可能破坏防止物种灭绝所需的合作。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Mining could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=infringe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>infringe</strong></a> protected boundaries, undermine habitat recovery and push a rare plant towards extinction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">采矿可能侵入保护边界、损害栖息地恢复，并将一种珍稀植物推向灭绝。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">infringe legal rights</span> · 侵犯合法权利</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=violation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">violation</a></span> · 违反</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=endangerment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">endangerment</a></span> · 濒危状态</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-114">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-114" class="vocabulary-word-title">undermine<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-114" aria-label="定位 undermine">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-114" data-mark-label="undermine" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="undermine：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="undermine：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undermine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 undermine" title="在有道查询 undermine">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-114</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 破坏；逐渐削弱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Illegal logging can infringe community rights, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undermine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undermine</strong></a> conservation and accelerate extinction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">非法采伐可能侵犯社区权利、损害保护工作并加速灭绝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Policies that infringe indigenous rights may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undermine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undermine</strong></a> cooperation needed to prevent extinction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">侵犯原住民权利的政策可能破坏防止物种灭绝所需的合作。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Mining could infringe protected boundaries, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undermine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undermine</strong></a> habitat recovery and push a rare plant towards extinction.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">采矿可能侵入保护边界、损害栖息地恢复，并将一种珍稀植物推向灭绝。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">undermine conservation</span> · 损害保护工作</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=violation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">violation</a></span> · 违反</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=endangerment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">endangerment</a></span> · 濒危状态</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-115">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-115" class="vocabulary-word-title">extinction<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-115" aria-label="定位 extinction">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-115" data-mark-label="extinction" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="extinction：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="extinction：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=extinction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 extinction" title="在有道查询 extinction">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-115</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 灭绝</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Illegal logging can infringe community rights, undermine conservation and accelerate <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=extinction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>extinction</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">非法采伐可能侵犯社区权利、损害保护工作并加速灭绝。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Policies that infringe indigenous rights may undermine cooperation needed to prevent <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=extinction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>extinction</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">侵犯原住民权利的政策可能破坏防止物种灭绝所需的合作。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Mining could infringe protected boundaries, undermine habitat recovery and push a rare plant towards <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=extinction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>extinction</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">采矿可能侵入保护边界、损害栖息地恢复，并将一种珍稀植物推向灭绝。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">species extinction</span> · 物种灭绝</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=violation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">violation</a></span> · 违反</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=endangerment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">endangerment</a></span> · 濒危状态</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 02.13｜描述实验与生产结果 {#group-02-13}

**串联方式：** 观察模式、影响和结果，再评价季节条件是否有利、实验是否有效、生产是否高效。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-116">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-116" class="vocabulary-word-title">pattern<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-116" aria-label="定位 pattern">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-116" data-mark-label="pattern" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pattern：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pattern：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pattern&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pattern" title="在有道查询 pattern">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-116</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 模式；式样；底样</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The experimental outcome revealed a seasonal growth <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pattern&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pattern</strong></a> and the impact of reduced rainfall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">实验结果揭示了季节性生长规律及降雨减少的影响。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared each experimental outcome to identify a seasonal <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pattern&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pattern</strong></a> in pollution's impact.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较各项实验结果，以识别污染影响中的季节性规律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The seasonal <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pattern&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pattern</strong></a> affected the experimental outcome, making the fertiliser's impact harder to isolate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季节性规律影响了实验结果，使肥料的作用更难单独识别。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an observable pattern</span> · 可观察的规律</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=observation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">observation</a></span> · 观察</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=result&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">result</a></span> · 结果</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-117">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-117" class="vocabulary-word-title">outcome<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-117" aria-label="定位 outcome">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-117" data-mark-label="outcome" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="outcome：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="outcome：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=outcome&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 outcome" title="在有道查询 outcome">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-117</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 结果</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The experimental <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=outcome&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>outcome</strong></a> revealed a seasonal growth pattern and the impact of reduced rainfall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">实验结果揭示了季节性生长规律及降雨减少的影响。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared each experimental <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=outcome&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>outcome</strong></a> to identify a seasonal pattern in pollution's impact.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较各项实验结果，以识别污染影响中的季节性规律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The seasonal pattern affected the experimental <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=outcome&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>outcome</strong></a>, making the fertiliser's impact harder to isolate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季节性规律影响了实验结果，使肥料的作用更难单独识别。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an experimental outcome</span> · 实验结果</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=observation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">observation</a></span> · 观察</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=result&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">result</a></span> · 结果</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-118">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-118" class="vocabulary-word-title">impact<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-118" aria-label="定位 impact">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-118" data-mark-label="impact" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="impact：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="impact：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=impact&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 impact" title="在有道查询 impact">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-118</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. 影响</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The experimental outcome revealed a seasonal growth pattern and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=impact&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>impact</strong></a> of reduced rainfall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">实验结果揭示了季节性生长规律及降雨减少的影响。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared each experimental outcome to identify a seasonal pattern in pollution's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=impact&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>impact</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较各项实验结果，以识别污染影响中的季节性规律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The seasonal pattern affected the experimental outcome, making the fertiliser's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=impact&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>impact</strong></a> harder to isolate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季节性规律影响了实验结果，使肥料的作用更难单独识别。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">environmental impact</span> · 环境影响</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=observation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">observation</a></span> · 观察</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=result&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">result</a></span> · 结果</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-119">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-119" class="vocabulary-word-title">seasonal<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-119" aria-label="定位 seasonal">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-119" data-mark-label="seasonal" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="seasonal：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="seasonal：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seasonal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 seasonal" title="在有道查询 seasonal">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-119</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 季节性的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The experimental outcome revealed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seasonal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>seasonal</strong></a> growth pattern and the impact of reduced rainfall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">实验结果揭示了季节性生长规律及降雨减少的影响。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared each experimental outcome to identify a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seasonal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>seasonal</strong></a> pattern in pollution's impact.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较各项实验结果，以识别污染影响中的季节性规律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seasonal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>seasonal</strong></a> pattern affected the experimental outcome, making the fertiliser's impact harder to isolate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季节性规律影响了实验结果，使肥料的作用更难单独识别。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">seasonal growth</span> · 季节性生长</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=observation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">observation</a></span> · 观察</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=result&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">result</a></span> · 结果</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-120">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-120" class="vocabulary-word-title">experimental<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-120" aria-label="定位 experimental">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-120" data-mark-label="experimental" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="experimental：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="experimental：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=experimental&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 experimental" title="在有道查询 experimental">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-120</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=experimental&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>experimental</strong></a> outcome revealed a seasonal growth pattern and the impact of reduced rainfall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">实验结果揭示了季节性生长规律及降雨减少的影响。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers compared each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=experimental&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>experimental</strong></a> outcome to identify a seasonal pattern in pollution's impact.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究人员比较各项实验结果，以识别污染影响中的季节性规律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The seasonal pattern affected the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=experimental&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>experimental</strong></a> outcome, making the fertiliser's impact harder to isolate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">季节性规律影响了实验结果，使肥料的作用更难单独识别。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">experimental evidence</span> · 实验证据</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=observation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">observation</a></span> · 观察</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=result&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">result</a></span> · 结果</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-121">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-121" class="vocabulary-word-title">favourable<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-121" aria-label="定位 favourable">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-121" data-mark-label="favourable" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="favourable：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="favourable：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=favourable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 favourable" title="在有道查询 favourable">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-121</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 有利的；赞成的，肯定的 (= favorable)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=favourable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Favourable</strong></a> weather and efficient irrigation made the land productive, while an effective spray controlled disease.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有利天气和高效灌溉使土地产量提高，有效喷剂则控制了病害。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An effective, efficient watering system keeps the soil productive even when conditions are less <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=favourable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>favourable</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有效且高效的浇灌系统即使在条件不太有利时，也能保持土壤生产力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The most productive plots combined <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=favourable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>favourable</strong></a> soil, effective pest control and efficient resource use.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">最高产的地块结合了有利土壤条件、有效病虫害防治和高效资源利用。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">favourable conditions</span> · 有利条件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=efficiency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">efficiency</a></span> · 效率</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=productivity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">productivity</a></span> · 生产力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-122">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-122" class="vocabulary-word-title">productive<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-122" aria-label="定位 productive">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-122" data-mark-label="productive" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="productive：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="productive：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=productive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 productive" title="在有道查询 productive">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-122</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 多产的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Favourable weather and efficient irrigation made the land <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=productive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>productive</strong></a>, while an effective spray controlled disease.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有利天气和高效灌溉使土地产量提高，有效喷剂则控制了病害。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An effective, efficient watering system keeps the soil <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=productive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>productive</strong></a> even when conditions are less favourable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有效且高效的浇灌系统即使在条件不太有利时，也能保持土壤生产力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The most <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=productive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>productive</strong></a> plots combined favourable soil, effective pest control and efficient resource use.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">最高产的地块结合了有利土壤条件、有效病虫害防治和高效资源利用。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">productive land</span> · 高产土地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=efficiency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">efficiency</a></span> · 效率</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=productivity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">productivity</a></span> · 生产力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-123">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-123" class="vocabulary-word-title">effective<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-123" aria-label="定位 effective">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-123" data-mark-label="effective" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="effective：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="effective：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=effective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 effective" title="在有道查询 effective">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-123</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 有效的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Favourable weather and efficient irrigation made the land productive, while an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=effective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>effective</strong></a> spray controlled disease.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有利天气和高效灌溉使土地产量提高，有效喷剂则控制了病害。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=effective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>effective</strong></a>, efficient watering system keeps the soil productive even when conditions are less favourable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有效且高效的浇灌系统即使在条件不太有利时，也能保持土壤生产力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The most productive plots combined favourable soil, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=effective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>effective</strong></a> pest control and efficient resource use.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">最高产的地块结合了有利土壤条件、有效病虫害防治和高效资源利用。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an effective treatment</span> · 有效处理方法</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=efficiency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">efficiency</a></span> · 效率</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=productivity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">productivity</a></span> · 生产力</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-124">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-124" class="vocabulary-word-title">efficient<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-124" aria-label="定位 efficient">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-124" data-mark-label="efficient" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="efficient：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="efficient：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=efficient&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 efficient" title="在有道查询 efficient">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-124</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Favourable weather and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=efficient&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>efficient</strong></a> irrigation made the land productive, while an effective spray controlled disease.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有利天气和高效灌溉使土地产量提高，有效喷剂则控制了病害。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An effective, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=efficient&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>efficient</strong></a> watering system keeps the soil productive even when conditions are less favourable.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有效且高效的浇灌系统即使在条件不太有利时，也能保持土壤生产力。</p></li>

### 词群 02.14｜描述规模与最优条件 {#group-02-14}

**串联方式：** 用大小、数量、上限、下限与最优状态描述实验条件，留意程度差异。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-125">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-125" class="vocabulary-word-title">considerable<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-125" aria-label="定位 considerable">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-125" data-mark-label="considerable" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="considerable：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="considerable：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=considerable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 considerable" title="在有道查询 considerable">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-125</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 相当多的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Massive deforestation caused <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=considerable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>considerable</strong></a> damage and placed immense pressure on surviving wildlife.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大规模毁林造成相当大的损害，并给幸存野生生物带来巨大压力。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Restoring this immense forest requires <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=considerable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>considerable</strong></a> funding after a massive fire.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一场大火之后，恢复这片辽阔森林需要相当多的资金。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The massive irrigation scheme brought <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=considerable&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>considerable</strong></a> benefits but consumed an immense amount of water.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大规模灌溉工程带来相当大的好处，但消耗了巨量水资源。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">considerable damage</span> · 相当大的损害</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">主题关联</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=substantial&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">substantial</a></span> · 大量的</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magnitude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">magnitude</a></span> · 规模</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="02-126">
<summary><h4 id="word-02-126" class="vocabulary-word-title">massive<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-02-126" aria-label="定位 massive">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="02-126" data-mark-label="massive" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="massive：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="massive：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=massive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 massive" title="在有道查询 massive">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">02-126</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj. 巨大的，大规模的，庞大的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=massive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Massive</strong></a> deforestation caused considerable damage and placed immense pressure on surviving wildlife.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大规模毁林造成相当大的损害，并给幸存野生生物带来巨大压力。</p></li>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **absorb nutrients**：吸收养分。
- **cultivate crops**：种植作物；crop 在第 12 章重现。
- **preserve biodiversity**：保护生物多样性；biodiversity 由 bio- 与 diversity 组成。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

A seed needs suitable conditions to germinate. As the plant grows, it absorbs water and nutrients and uses sunlight for photosynthesis. It may then flower and produce fruit. Farmers cultivate plants to obtain a harvest, while conservation work helps preserve the diversity of wild plants.

**中文理解：** 种子需要合适条件才能萌发。植物生长时吸收水和养分，并利用阳光进行光合作用，之后可能开花结果。农民栽培植物以获得收成，保护工作则有助于保存野生植物的多样性。

### 闭卷回忆｜先尝试，再逐级提示

**任务：** 从一粒种子开始，解释它怎样生长、被栽培，并受到环境影响。

1. 关掉本章正文，在空白页列出你记得的词群；每群先写 3—5 个英文词或搭配。
2. 从中选 3 个词群，连成 3—5 句英文；用明确关系连接，专业分类和事实不确定时先核对。
3. 卡住时，先只看“本章主线”；还想不起，再看词群标题和中文简释，最后才看英文。
4. 用核对编号找出遗漏词；把“见到认得”与“想表达时能说出”分开记录。
5. 合上提示重新说一遍；下次换一个对象、地点或例子，检查能否迁移。
